## Competition Act {#competition-act .Title-of-Act}

[R.S.C.]{.abbr title="Revised Statutes of Canada"}, 1985, c. C-34
:::

An Act to provide for the general regulation of trade and commerce in
respect of conspiracies, trade practices and mergers affecting
competition
::::

::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-87824 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Short title

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** This Act may be cited as
the [Competition Act](/eng/acts/C-34).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 1
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 19
:::

## [PART I]{.HLabel1}[Purpose and Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-87829 .Part}

### [Purpose]{.HTitleText2} {#h-87830 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purpose of Act

**[[1.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1.1 .sectionLabel}** The purpose of this Act
is to maintain and encourage competition in Canada in order to promote
the efficiency and adaptability of the Canadian economy, in order to
expand opportunities for Canadian participation in world markets while
at the same time recognizing the role of foreign competition in Canada,
in order to ensure that small and medium-sized enterprises have an
equitable opportunity to participate in the Canadian economy and in
order to provide consumers with competitive prices and product choices.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 19
:::

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-87835 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In this
  Act,

  [[article]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[article]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means real and personal property
      of every description including

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} money,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} deeds and instruments relating to or evidencing
        the title or right to property or an interest, immediate,
        contingent or otherwise, in a corporation or in any assets of a
        corporation,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} deeds and instruments giving a right to recover
        or receive property,

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} tickets or like evidence of right to be in
        attendance at a particular place at a particular time or times
        or of a right to transportation, and

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} energy, however
        generated; ([article]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes the business of

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} manufacturing, producing, transporting,
        acquiring, supplying, storing and otherwise dealing in articles,
        and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} acquiring, supplying and otherwise dealing in
        services.

      It also includes the raising of funds for charitable or other
      non-profit purposes. ([entreprise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Commission]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Commission]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, R.S., 1985, c. 19
      (2nd Supp.), s. 20\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Commissioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Commissioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Commissioner of
      Competition appointed under subsection
      7(1); ([commissaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[computer system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[computer system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 342.1(2) of the [Criminal
      Code](/eng/acts/C-46); ([ordinateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[data]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[data]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means representations, including
      signs, signals or symbols, that are capable of being understood by
      an individual or processed by a computer system or other
      device; ([données]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 1999, c. 2, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[electronic message]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[electronic message]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a message sent by
      any means of telecommunication, including a text, sound, voice or
      image message; ([message électronique]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a corporation or a
      partnership, sole proprietorship, trust or other unincorporated
      organization capable of conducting
      business; ([entité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[information]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[information]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes
      data; ([renseignement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[locator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[locator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a name or information used
      to identify a source of data on a computer system, and includes a
      URL; ([localisateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[merger]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[merger]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd
      Supp.), s. 20\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of
      Industry; ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[monopoly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[monopoly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, R.S., 1985, c. 19
      (2nd Supp.), s. 20\]]{.Repealed}

  [[product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes an article and a
      service; ([produit]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[record]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[record]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a medium on which information
      is registered or marked; ([document]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[sender information]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sender information]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the part of an
      electronic message --- including the data relating to source,
      routing, addressing or signalling --- that identifies or purports
      to identify the sender or the origin of the
      message; ([renseignements sur l'expéditeur]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a service of any description
      whether industrial, trade, professional or
      otherwise; ([service]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[subject matter information]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[subject matter information]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the part
      of an electronic message that purports to summarize the contents
      of the message or to give an indication of
      them; ([objet]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[supply]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[supply]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means,

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in relation to an article, sell, rent, lease or
        otherwise dispose of an article or an interest therein or a
        right thereto, or offer so to dispose of an article or interest
        therein or a right thereto, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a service, sell, rent or
        otherwise provide a service or offer so to provide a
        service; ([fournir]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou
        [approvisionner]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[trade, industry or profession]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[trade, industry or profession]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes any
      class, division or branch of a trade, industry or
      profession; ([commerce, industrie ou profession]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Tribunal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Tribunal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Competition Tribunal
      established by subsection 3(1) of the [Competition Tribunal
      Act](/eng/acts/C-36.4). ([Tribunal]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Affiliation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} one entity is affiliated with another entity if one
    of them is the subsidiary of the other or both are subsidiaries of
    the same entity or each of them is controlled by the same entity or
    individual;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if two entities are affiliated with the same entity
    at the same time, they are deemed to be affiliated with each other;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an individual is affiliated with an entity if the
    individual controls the entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsidiary entity

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, an entity is a
  subsidiary of another entity if it is controlled by that other entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Control

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a corporation is controlled by an entity or an
    individual other than Her Majesty if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} securities of the corporation to which are
      attached more than 50% of the votes that may be cast to elect
      directors of the corporation are held, directly or indirectly,
      whether through one or more subsidiaries or otherwise, otherwise
      than by way of security only, by or for the benefit of that entity
      or individual, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the votes attached to those securities are
      sufficient, if exercised, to elect a majority of the directors of
      the corporation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a corporation is controlled by Her Majesty in right
    of Canada or a province if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the corporation is controlled by Her Majesty in
      the manner described in paragraph (a), or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a corporation without share
      capital, a majority of the directors of the corporation, other
      than [ex officio]{.otherLang lang="la"} directors, are appointed
      by

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the Governor in Council or the Lieutenant
        Governor in Council of the province, as the case may be, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} a Minister of the government of Canada or the
        province, as the case may be; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an entity other than a corporation is controlled by
    an entity or individual if the entity or individual, directly or
    indirectly, whether through one or more subsidiaries or otherwise,
    holds an interest in the entity that is not a corporation that
    entitles them to receive more than 50% of the profits of that entity
    or more than 50% of its assets on dissolution.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 2
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 20
- 1992, c. 1, s. 145(F)
- 1995, c. 1, s. 62
- 1999, c. 2, s. 1, c. 31, s. 44(F)
- 2010, c. 23, s. 70
- 2014, c. 31, ss. 28, 46
- 2018, c. 8, s. 109
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-2-20150309.html "Link to previous version of section 2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Binding on agents of Her Majesty in
certain cases

**[[2.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.1 .sectionLabel}** This Act is binding on
and applies to an agent of Her Majesty in right of Canada or a province
that is a corporation, in respect of commercial activities engaged in by
the corporation in competition, whether actual or potential, with other
persons to the extent that it would apply if the agent were not an agent
of Her Majesty.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 21
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Defects of form

**[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** No proceedings under this
Act shall be deemed invalid by reason of any defect of form or any
technical irregularity.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. C-23, s. 3
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Collective bargaining activities

- **[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Nothing
  in this Act applies in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} combinations or activities of workmen or employees
    for their own reasonable protection as such workmen or employees;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} contracts, agreements or arrangements between or
    among fishermen or associations of fishermen and persons or
    associations of persons engaged in the buying or processing of fish
    relating to the prices, remuneration or other like conditions under
    which fish will be caught and supplied to those persons by
    fishermen; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} contracts, agreements or arrangements between or
    among two or more employers in a trade, industry or profession,
    whether effected directly between or among the employers or through
    the instrumentality of a corporation or association of which the
    employers are members, pertaining to collective bargaining with
    their employees in respect of salary or wages and terms or
    conditions of employment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in this section exempts from the application
  of any provision of this Act a contract, agreement or arrangement
  entered into by an employer to withhold any product from any person,
  or to refrain from acquiring from any person any product other than
  the services of workmen or employees.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. C-23, s. 4
- 1974-75-76, c. 76, s. 2
:::

**[[4.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4.1 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2009, c.
2, s. 407\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-4.1-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 4.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Underwriters

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Section
  45 does not apply in respect of an agreement or arrangement between
  persons who are members of a class of persons who ordinarily engage in
  the business of dealing in securities or between such persons and the
  issuer of a specific security, in the case of a primary distribution,
  or the vendor of a specific security, in the case of a secondary
  distribution, if the agreement or arrangement has a reasonable
  relationship to the underwriting of a specific security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[underwriting]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section,
  [[underwriting]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a security means the primary
  or secondary distribution of the security, in respect of which
  distribution

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a prospectus is required to be filed, accepted or
    otherwise approved pursuant to a law enacted in Canada or in a
    jurisdiction outside Canada for the supervision or regulation of
    trade in securities; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prospectus would be required to be filed,
    accepted or otherwise approved but for an express exemption
    contained in or given pursuant to a law mentioned in paragraph (a).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 5
- 1999, c. 2, s. 2
- 2009, c. 2, s. 408
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-5-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 5"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amateur sport

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This Act
  does not apply in respect of agreements or arrangements between or
  among teams, clubs and leagues pertaining to participation in amateur
  sport.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[amateur
  sport]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, [[amateur
  sport]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means sport in which the participants
  receive no remuneration for their services as participants.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1974-75-76, c. 76, s. 2
:::

## [PART II]{.HLabel1}[Administration]{.HTitleText1} {#h-87926 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Commissioner of Competition

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may appoint an officer to be known as the
  Commissioner of Competition, who shall be responsible for

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the administration and enforcement of this Act; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the administration and enforcement of the [Consumer
    Packaging and Labelling Act](/eng/acts/C-38), the [Precious Metals
    Marking Act](/eng/acts/P-19) and the [Textile Labelling
    Act](/eng/acts/T-10).

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2012, c. 24, s. 79\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2012, c. 24, s. 79\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Oath of office

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner shall, before taking up the duties
  of the Commissioner, take and subscribe, before the Clerk of the Privy
  Council, an oath or solemn affirmation, which shall be filed in the
  office of the Clerk, in the following form:

  I do solemnly swear (or affirm) that I will faithfully, truly and
  impartially, and to the best of my judgment, skill and ability,
  execute the powers and trusts reposed in me as Commissioner of
  Competition. (*In the case where an oath is taken add* "So help me
  God".)

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Salary

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner shall be paid such salary as may be
  from time to time fixed and allowed by the Governor in Council.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 7
- 1999, c. 2, ss. 4, 37
- [2012, c. 24, s. 79](#1033666-1109984){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1033666-1109984.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-7-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 7"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deputy Commissioners

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} One or
  more persons may be appointed Deputy Commissioners of Competition in
  the manner authorized by law.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of Deputy

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may authorize a Deputy
  Commissioner to exercise the powers and perform the duties of the
  Commissioner whenever the Commissioner is absent or unable to act or
  whenever there is a vacancy in the office of Commissioner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of other persons

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may authorize any person to
  exercise the powers and perform the duties of the Commissioner
  whenever the Commissioner and the Deputy Commissioners are absent or
  unable to act or, if one or more of those offices are vacant, whenever
  the holders of the other of those offices are absent or unable to act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inquiry by Deputy

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner may authorize a Deputy Commissioner
  to make inquiry regarding any matter into which the Commissioner has
  power to inquire, and when so authorized a Deputy Commissioner shall
  perform the duties and may exercise the powers of the Commissioner in
  respect of that matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of Commissioner unaffected

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The exercise, pursuant to this Act, of any of the
  powers or the performance of any of the duties of the Commissioner by
  a Deputy Commissioner or other person does not in any way limit,
  restrict or qualify the powers or duties of the Commissioner, either
  generally or with respect to any particular matter.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 8
- 1999, c. 2, s. 5
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for inquiry

- **[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any six
  persons resident in Canada who are not less than eighteen years of age
  and who are of the opinion that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person has contravened an order made pursuant to
    section 32, 33 or 34, or Part VII.1 or VIII,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} grounds exist for the making of an order under Part
    VII.1 or VIII, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an offence under Part VI or VII has been or is
    about to be committed,

  may apply to the Commissioner for an inquiry into the matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Material to be submitted

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An application made under subsection (1) shall be
  accompanied by a statement in the form of a solemn or statutory
  declaration showing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the names and addresses of the applicants, and at
    their election the name and address of any one of their number, or
    of any attorney, solicitor or counsel, whom they may, for the
    purpose of receiving any communication to be made pursuant to this
    Act, have authorized to represent them;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the nature of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the alleged contravention,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the grounds alleged to exist for the making of
      an order, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the alleged offence

    and the names of the persons believed to be concerned therein and
    privy thereto; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a concise statement of the evidence supporting
    their opinion.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 9
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 22
- 1999, c. 2, ss. 6, 37
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inquiry by Commissioner

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commissioner shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on application made under section 9,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} whenever the Commissioner has reason to believe
    that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a person has contravened an order made pursuant
      to section 32, 33 or 34, or Part VII.1 or Part VIII,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} grounds exist for the making of an order under
      Part VII.1 or Part VIII, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an offence under Part VI or VII has been or is
      about to be committed, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} whenever directed by the Minister to inquire
    whether any of the circumstances described in subparagraphs (b)(i)
    to (iii) exists,

  cause an inquiry to be made into all such matters as the Commissioner
  considers necessary to inquire into with the view of determining the
  facts.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information on inquiry

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner shall, on the written request of any
  person whose conduct is being inquired into under this Act or any
  person who applies for an inquiry under section 9, inform that person
  or cause that person to be informed as to the progress of the inquiry.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inquiries to be in private

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} All inquiries under this section shall be conducted
  in private.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 10
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 23
- 1999, c. 2, ss. 7, 37, c. 31, s. 45
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Market or industry inquiry

- **[[10.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner may, after
  consulting the Minister, conduct an inquiry into the state of
  competition in a market or industry if the Commissioner is of the
  opinion that it is in the public interest to do so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Direction to conduct inquiry

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may direct the Commissioner to conduct
  an inquiry into the state of competition in a market or industry if
  the Minister is of the opinion that it is in the public interest that
  such an inquiry be conducted. Before making the direction, the
  Minister must consult the Commissioner to determine whether the
  inquiry would be feasible, including with regard to its cost.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proposed terms of reference

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If, after the consultation referred to in
  subsection (1) or (2), it is decided to proceed with the inquiry, the
  Commissioner must prepare proposed terms of reference for the inquiry
  and publish them on a publicly available website and invite the public
  to provide comments during a period of not less than 15 days.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Final terms of reference

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} After having taken into account any comments received
  from the public, the Commissioner must submit to the Minister for
  approval the Commissioner's final terms of reference and, if they are
  approved, the Commissioner must publish them on a publicly available
  website.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duration of inquiry

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The inquiry commences on the day on which the final
  terms of reference are published and the Commissioner must complete
  the inquiry and publish a report of the Commissioner's findings on a
  publicly available website before the expiry of the period specified
  by the Minister, which period, subject to subsection (6), is not to
  exceed 18 months.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may extend the specified period for
  periods of up to three months.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sending draft to certain persons

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Before the report is published, the Commissioner must
  send to every person who was required to do anything under an order
  made under subsection 11(1) a complete or partial draft of the report
  and inform the person that they may, within three working days after
  the day on which it was sent, provide the Commissioner with the
  person's concerns regarding factual inaccuracies or confidential
  information that should not be disclosed in the final report.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 31, s. 3](#1431653-1432199){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1431653-1432199.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order for oral examination, production or
written return

- **[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If, on
  the [ex parte]{.otherLang lang="la"} application of the Commissioner
  or his or her authorized representative, a judge of a superior or
  county court is satisfied by information on oath or solemn affirmation
  that an inquiry is being made under section 10 or 10.1 and that a
  person has or is likely to have information that is relevant to the
  inquiry, the judge may order the person to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} attend as specified in the order and be examined on
    oath or solemn affirmation by the Commissioner or the authorized
    representative of the Commissioner on any matter that is relevant to
    the inquiry before a person, in this section and sections 12 to 14
    referred to as a "presiding officer", designated in the order;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} produce to the Commissioner or the authorized
    representative of the Commissioner within a time and at a place
    specified in the order, a record, a copy of a record certified by
    affidavit to be a true copy, or any other thing, specified in the
    order; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} make and deliver to the Commissioner or the
    authorized representative of the Commissioner, within a time
    specified in the order, a written return under oath or solemn
    affirmation showing in detail such information as is by the order
    required.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Records or information in possession of
  affiliate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the person against whom an order is sought under
  paragraph (1)(b) or (c) in relation to an inquiry is a corporation and
  the judge to whom the application is made under subsection (1) is
  satisfied by information on oath or solemn affirmation that an
  affiliate of the corporation, whether the affiliate is located in
  Canada or outside Canada, has or is likely to have records or
  information relevant to the inquiry, the judge may order the
  corporation to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} produce the records; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} make and deliver a written return of the
    information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No person excused from complying with
  order

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall be excused from complying with an
  order under subsection (1) or (2) on the ground that the testimony,
  record or other thing or return required of the person may tend to
  criminate the person or subject him to any proceeding or penalty, but
  no testimony given by an individual pursuant to an order made under
  paragraph (1)(a), or return made by an individual pursuant to an order
  made under paragraph (1)(c), shall be used or received against that
  individual in any criminal proceedings thereafter instituted against
  him, other than a prosecution under section 132 or 136 of the
  [Criminal Code](/eng/acts/C-46).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of order

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An order made under this section has effect anywhere
  in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Person outside Canada

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} An order may be made under subsection (1) against a
  person outside Canada who carries on business in Canada or sells
  products into Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 11
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 24
- 1999, c. 2, s. 37
- 2002, c. 8, s. 126, c. 16, s. 1
- [2022, c. 10, s. 256](#1357320-1366432){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357320-1366432.html"}
- [2023, c. 31, s. 4](#1431655-1432203){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1431655-1432203.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11-20220623.html "Link to previous version of section 11"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Witness competent and compellable

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  person summoned to attend pursuant to paragraph 11(1)(a) is competent
  and may be compelled to give evidence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fees

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person summoned to attend pursuant to paragraph
  11(1)(a) is entitled to the like fees and allowances for so doing as
  if summoned to attend before a superior court of the province in which
  the person is summoned to attend.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representation by counsel

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A presiding officer shall permit a person who is
  being examined pursuant to an order under paragraph 11(1)(a) and any
  person whose conduct is being inquired into to be represented by
  counsel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Attendance of person whose conduct is
  being inquired into

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Any person whose conduct is being inquired into at an
  examination pursuant to an order under paragraph 11(1)(a) and that
  person's counsel are entitled to attend the examination unless the
  Commissioner or the authorized representative of the Commissioner, or
  the person being examined or his employer, establishes to the
  satisfaction of the presiding officer that the presence of the person
  whose conduct is being inquired into would

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be prejudicial to the effective conduct of the
    examination or the inquiry; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} result in the disclosure of confidential commercial
    information that relates to the business of the person being
    examined or his employer.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 12
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 24
- 1999, c. 2, s. 37
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presiding officer

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  person may be designated as a presiding officer who is a barrister or
  advocate of at least ten years standing at the bar of a province or
  who has been a barrister or advocate at the bar of a province for at
  least ten years.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Remuneration and expenses

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A presiding officer shall be paid such remuneration,
  and is entitled to be paid such travel and living expenses, and such
  other expenses, incurred in the performance of his duties under this
  Act, as may be fixed by the Governor in Council.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 13
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 24
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Administration of oaths

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  presiding officer may administer oaths and take and receive solemn
  affirmations for the purposes of examinations pursuant to paragraph
  11(1)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Orders of presiding officer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A presiding officer may make such orders as he
  considers to be proper for the conduct of an examination pursuant to
  paragraph 11(1)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to court

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A judge of a superior or county court may, on
  application by a presiding officer, order any person to comply with an
  order made by the presiding officer under subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} No order may be made under subsection (3) unless the
  presiding officer has given to the person in respect of whom the order
  is sought and the Commissioner twenty-four hours notice of the hearing
  of the application for the order or such shorter notice as the judge
  to whom the application is made considers reasonable.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 14
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 24
- 1999, c. 2, s. 37
- 2002, c. 8, s. 127
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-14-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 14"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of [Criminal
Code](/eng/acts/C-46) --- preservation demand and orders for
preservation or production of data

- **[[14.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Sections 487.012, 487.013, 487.015,
  487.016 and 487.018 of the [Criminal Code](/eng/acts/C-46), which
  apply to the investigation of offences under any Act of Parliament,
  also apply, with any modifications that the circumstances require,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to an investigation in relation to a contravention
    of an order made under section 32, 33 or 34 or Part VII.1 or VIII;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to an investigation in relation to whether grounds
    exist for the making of an order under Part VII.1 or VIII.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clarification

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The provisions of the [Criminal Code](/eng/acts/C-46)
  referred to in subsection (1) apply whether or not an inquiry has been
  commenced under section 10.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 31, s. 29
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Warrant for entry of premises

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If, on
  the [ex parte]{.otherLang lang="la"} application of the Commissioner
  or his or her authorized representative, a judge of a superior or
  county court is satisfied by information on oath or solemn affirmation

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that there are reasonable grounds to believe that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a person has contravened an order made pursuant
      to section 32, 33 or 34, or Part VII.1 or VIII,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} grounds exist for the making of an order under
      Part VII.1 or VIII, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an offence under Part VI or VII has been or is
      about to be committed, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that there are reasonable grounds to believe that
    there is, on any premises, any record or other thing that will
    afford evidence with respect to the circumstances referred to in
    subparagraph (a)(i), (ii) or (iii), as the case may be,

  the judge may issue a warrant under his hand authorizing the
  Commissioner or any other person named in the warrant to

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} enter the premises, subject to such conditions as
    may be specified in the warrant, and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} search the premises for any such record or other
    thing and copy it or seize it for examination or copying.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of warrant

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A warrant issued under this section shall identify
  the matter in respect of which it is issued, the premises to be
  searched and the record or other thing, or the class of records or
  other things, to be searched for.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Execution of search warrant

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A warrant issued under this section shall be executed
  between six o'clock in the forenoon and nine o'clock in the afternoon,
  unless the judge issuing it, by the warrant, authorizes execution of
  it at another time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A warrant issued under this section may be executed
  anywhere in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty of persons in control of premises

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Every person who is in possession or control of any
  premises or record or other thing in respect of which a warrant is
  issued under subsection (1) shall, on presentation of the warrant,
  permit the Commissioner or other person named in the warrant to enter
  the premises, search the premises and examine the record or other
  thing and to copy it or seize it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where admission or access refused

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Where the Commissioner or any other person, in
  executing a warrant issued under subsection (1), is refused access to
  any premises, record or other thing or where the Commissioner believes
  on reasonable grounds that access will be refused, the judge who
  issued the warrant or a judge of the same court, on the [ex
  parte]{.otherLang lang="la"} application of the Commissioner, may by
  order direct a peace officer to take such steps as the judge considers
  necessary to give the Commissioner or other person access.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where warrant not necessary

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner or the authorized representative of
  the Commissioner may exercise any of the powers set out in paragraph
  (1)(c) or (d) without a warrant if the conditions set out in
  paragraphs (1)(a) and (b) exist but by reason of exigent circumstances
  it would not be practical to obtain the warrant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exigent circumstances

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (7), exigent
  circumstances include circumstances in which the delay necessary to
  obtain a warrant under subsection (1) would result in the loss or
  destruction of evidence.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 15
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 24
- 1999, c. 2, ss. 8, 37
- 2002, c. 8, s. 128
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-15-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 15"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Operation of computer system

- **[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who is authorized pursuant to subsection 15(1) to search
  premises for a record may use or cause to be used any computer system
  on the premises to search any data contained in or available to the
  computer system, may reproduce the record or cause it to be reproduced
  from the data in the form of a printout or other intelligible output
  and may seize the printout or other output for examination or copying.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty of person in control of computer
  system

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who is in possession or control of any
  premises in respect of which a warrant is issued under subsection
  15(1) shall, on presentation of the warrant, permit any person named
  in the warrant to use or cause to be used any computer system or part
  thereof on the premises to search any data contained in or available
  to the computer system for data from which a record that that person
  is authorized to search for may be produced, to obtain a physical copy
  thereof and to seize it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order restricting operation of computer
  system

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A judge who issued a warrant under subsection 15(1)
  or a judge of the same court may, on application by the Commissioner
  or any person who is in possession or control of a computer system or
  a part thereof on any premises in respect of which the warrant was
  issued, make an order

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} specifying the individuals who may operate the
    computer system and fixing the times when they may do so; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} setting out any other terms and conditions on which
    the computer system may be operated.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice by person in possession or
  control

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} No order may be made under subsection (3) on
  application by a person who is in possession or control of a computer
  system or part thereof unless that person has given the Commissioner
  twenty-four hours notice of the hearing of the application or such
  shorter notice as the judge considers reasonable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice by Commissioner

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} No order may be made under subsection (3) on
  application by the Commissioner after a search has begun of the
  premises in respect of which the order is sought unless the
  Commissioner has given the person who is in possession or control of
  the premises twenty-four hours notice of the hearing of the
  application or such shorter notice as the judge considers reasonable.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2010, c. 23, s. 71\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 16
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 24
- 1999, c. 2, s. 37
- 2010, c. 23, s. 71
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-16-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 16"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presentation of or report on record or
thing seized

- **[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  a record or other thing is seized pursuant to paragraph 15(1)(d),
  subsection 15(7) or section 16, the Commissioner or the authorized
  representative of the Commissioner shall, as soon as practicable,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} take the record or other thing before the judge who
    issued the warrant or a judge of the same court or, if no warrant
    was issued, before a judge of a superior or county court; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} make a report in respect of the record or other
    thing to a judge determined in accordance with paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A report to a judge under paragraph (1)(b) in respect
  of a record or other thing shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a statement as to whether the record or other thing
    was seized pursuant to paragraph 15(1)(d), subsection 15(7) or
    section 16;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a description of the premises searched;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a description of the record or other thing seized;
    and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the location in which it is detained.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention or return of thing seized

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a record or other thing is seized pursuant to
  section 15 or 16, the judge before whom it is taken or to whom a
  report is made in respect of it pursuant to this section may, if he is
  satisfied that the record or other thing is required for an inquiry or
  any proceeding under this Act, authorize the Commissioner to retain
  it.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 17
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 24
- 1999, c. 2, s. 37, c. 31, s. 46(F)
- 2002, c. 8, s. 129
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-17-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 17"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Commissioner to take reasonable care

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  any record or other thing is produced pursuant to section 11 or seized
  pursuant to section 15 or 16, the Commissioner shall take reasonable
  care to ensure that it is preserved until it is returned to the person
  by whom it was produced or from whom it was seized or until it is
  required to be produced in any proceeding under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certified copies

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner need not return any copy of a
  record produced under section 11 or obtained under section 15 or 16.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access to records or things

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The person by whom a record or other thing is
  produced pursuant to section 11 or from whom a record or other thing
  is seized pursuant to section 15 or 16 is entitled, at any reasonable
  time and subject to such reasonable conditions as may be imposed by
  the Commissioner, to inspect the record or other thing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy of record where returned

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner may, before returning any record
  produced pursuant to section 11 or seized pursuant to section 15 or
  16, make or cause to be made, and may retain, a copy thereof.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Detention of things seized

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Any record or other thing that is produced pursuant
  to section 11, or the retention of which is authorized under
  subsection 17(3), shall be returned to the person by whom it was
  produced or the person from whom it was seized not later than sixty
  days after it was produced or its retention was authorized, unless,
  before the expiration of that period,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person by whom it was produced or from whom it
    was seized agrees to its further detention for a specified period of
    time;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the judge who authorized its production or
    retention or a judge of the same court is satisfied on application
    that, having regard to the circumstances, its further detention for
    a specified period of time is warranted and the judge so orders; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} proceedings are instituted in which the record or
    thing may be required.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 18
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 24
- 1999, c. 2, s. 37
- 2002, c. 16, s. 2
- 2017, c. 26, s. 12
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-18-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 18"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Claim to solicitor-client privilege
(section 11)

- **[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  a person is ordered to produce a record pursuant to section 11 and
  that person claims that there exists a solicitor-client privilege in
  respect thereof, the person shall place it in a package and seal and
  identify the package and place it in the custody of a person referred
  to in subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Claim to solicitor-client privilege
  (section 15 or 16)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where, pursuant to section 15 or 16, any person is
  about to examine, copy or seize or is in the course of examining,
  copying or seizing any record and a person appearing to be in
  authority claims that there exists a solicitor-client privilege in
  respect thereof, the first-mentioned person, unless the person
  claiming the privilege withdraws the claim or the first-mentioned
  person desists from examining and copying the record and from seizing
  it or a copy thereof, shall, without examining or further examining it
  or making a copy or further copy thereof, place it and any copies of
  it made by him, and any notes taken in respect of it, in a package,
  and seal and identify the package and place it in the custody of a
  person referred to in subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Custody of record

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A record in respect of which a solicitor-client
  privilege is claimed under subsection (1) or (2) shall be placed in
  the custody of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the registrar, prothonotary or other like officer
    of a superior or county court in the province in which the record
    was ordered to be produced or in which it was found, or of the
    Federal Court;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a sheriff of the district or county in which the
    record was ordered to be produced or in which it was found; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} some person agreed on between the Commissioner or
    the authorized representative of the Commissioner and the person who
    makes the claim of privilege.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of claim to privilege

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A judge of a superior or county court in the province
  in which a record placed in custody under this section was ordered to
  be produced or in which it was found, or of the Federal Court, sitting
  [in camera]{.otherLang lang="la"}, may decide the question of
  solicitor-client privilege in relation to the record on application
  made in accordance with the rules of the court by the Commissioner or
  the owner of the record or the person in whose possession it was found
  if notice of the application has been given by the applicant to all
  other persons entitled to make application.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2024, c. 15, s. 231\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authority of judge

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A judge referred to in subsection (4) may give any
  directions that the judge deems necessary to give effect to this
  section, may order delivery up to the judge out of custody of any
  record in respect of which he is asked to decide a question of
  solicitor-client privilege and may inspect any such record.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Any person who is about to examine, copy or seize any
  record pursuant to section 15 or 16 shall not do so without affording
  a reasonable opportunity for a claim of solicitor-client privilege to
  be made under this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access to record in custody

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} At any time while a record is in custody under this
  section, a judge of a superior or county court in the province in
  which the record is in custody, or of the Federal Court, may, on an
  [ex parte]{.otherLang lang="la"} application of a person claiming
  solicitor-client privilege under this section, authorize that person
  to examine the record or make a copy of it in the presence of the
  person who has custody of it or the judge, but any such authorization
  shall contain provisions to ensure that the record is repackaged and
  that the package is resealed without alteration or damage.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 19
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 24
- 1999, c. 2, s. 37
- [2024, c. 15, s. 231](#1462789-1484015){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1462789-1484015.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-19-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 19"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspection of records and things

- **[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} All
  records or other things obtained or received by the Commissioner may
  be inspected by the Commissioner and also by such persons as he
  directs.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Copies of any records referred to in subsection (1),
  made by any process of reproduction, on proof orally or by affidavit
  that they are true copies, are admissible in evidence in any
  proceedings under this Act and have the same probative force as the
  original.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where proof referred to in subsection (2) is offered
  by affidavit, it is not necessary to prove the signature or official
  character of the deponent, if that information is set out in the
  affidavit, or to prove the signature or official character of the
  person before whom the affidavit was sworn.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 20
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 24
- 1999, c. 2, s. 37
- 2010, c. 23, s. 72
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-20-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 20"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Counsel

**[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** Whenever in the opinion
of the Commissioner the public interest so requires, the Commissioner
may apply to the Attorney General of Canada to appoint and instruct
counsel to assist in an inquiry under section 10 or 10.1, and on such an
application the Attorney General of Canada may appoint and instruct
counsel accordingly.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 21
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 24
- 1999, c. 2, s. 37
- [2023, c. 31, s. 5](#1431657-1432206){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1431657-1432206.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-21-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 21"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Discontinuance of inquiry

- **[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} At any
  stage of an inquiry under section 10, if the Commissioner is of the
  opinion that the matter being inquired into does not justify further
  inquiry, the Commissioner may discontinue the inquiry.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner shall, on discontinuing an inquiry,
  make a report in writing to the Minister showing the information
  obtained and the reason for discontinuing the inquiry.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to applicant

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where an inquiry made on application under section 9
  is discontinued, the Commissioner shall inform the applicants of the
  decision and give the grounds therefor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review of decision

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, on the written request of
  applicants under section 9 or on the Minister's own motion, review any
  decision of the Commissioner to discontinue an inquiry under section
  10, and may, if in the Minister's opinion the circumstances warrant,
  instruct the Commissioner to make further inquiry.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 22
- R.S., 1985, c. 27 (1st Supp.), s. 187, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 24
- 1999, c. 2, s. 37, c. 31, s. 47(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reference to Attorney General of Canada

- **[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commissioner may, at any stage of an inquiry under section 10, in
  addition to or in lieu of continuing the inquiry, remit any records,
  returns or evidence to the Attorney General of Canada for
  consideration as to whether an offence has been or is about to be
  committed against this Act and for such action as the Attorney General
  of Canada may wish to take.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prosecution by Attorney General of
  Canada

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Attorney General of Canada may institute and
  conduct any prosecution or other criminal proceedings under this Act,
  and for those purposes may exercise all the powers and perform all the
  duties and functions conferred by the [Criminal Code](/eng/acts/C-46)
  on the attorney general of a province.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 23
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 24
- 1999, c. 2, s. 37
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may make regulations regulating the practice and
  procedure in respect of applications, proceedings and orders under
  sections 11 to 19.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication of proposed regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), a copy of each regulation
  that the Governor in Council proposes to make under subsection (1)
  shall be published in the [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/)
  at least sixty days before the proposed effective date thereof and a
  reasonable opportunity shall be afforded to interested persons to make
  representations with respect thereto.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No proposed regulation need be published under
  subsection (2) if it has previously been published pursuant to that
  subsection, whether or not it has been amended as a result of
  representations made pursuant to that subsection.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 24
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 24
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Staff

**[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** All officers, clerks and
employees required for carrying out this Act shall be appointed in
accordance with the [Public Service Employment Act](/eng/acts/P-33.01),
except that the Commissioner may, with the approval of the Governor in
Council, employ such temporary, technical and special assistants as may
be required to meet the special conditions that may arise in carrying
out this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 25
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 25
- 1999, c. 2, s. 37
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Remuneration of temporary staff

- **[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  temporary, technical and special assistants employed by the
  Commissioner shall be paid such remuneration, and are entitled to be
  paid such travel and living expenses incurred in the performance of
  their duties under this Act, as may be fixed by the Governor in
  Council.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Remuneration and expenses payable out
  of appropriations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The remuneration and expenses of the Commissioner and
  of the temporary, technical and special assistants employed by the
  Commissioner, and of any counsel instructed under this Act, shall be
  paid out of money appropriated by Parliament to defray the cost of
  administering this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Public Service Employment
  Act](/eng/acts/P-33.01) applies

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this section and section 7, the [Public
  Service Employment Act](/eng/acts/P-33.01) and other Acts relating to
  the public service, in so far as applicable, apply to the Commissioner
  and to all other persons employed under this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 26
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 25
- 1999, c. 2, s. 37
- 2003, c. 22, s. 225(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-26-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 26"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authority of technical or special
assistants

**[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** Any technical or special
assistant or other person employed under this Act, when so authorized or
deputed by the Commissioner, has power and authority to exercise any of
the powers and perform any of the duties of the Commissioner under this
Act with respect to any particular inquiry, as may be directed by the
Commissioner.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 27
- 1999, c. 2, s. 37
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister may require interim report

**[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may at any
time require the Commissioner to submit an interim report with respect
to any inquiry by him under this Act, and it is the duty of the
Commissioner whenever thereunto required by the Minister to render an
interim report setting out the action taken, the evidence obtained and
the Commissioner's opinion as to the effect of the evidence.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 28
- 1999, c. 2, s. 37
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Confidentiality

- **[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person who performs or has performed duties or functions in the
  administration or enforcement of this Act shall communicate or allow
  to be communicated to any other person except to a Canadian law
  enforcement agency or for the purposes of the administration or
  enforcement of this Act

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the identity of any person from whom information
    was obtained pursuant to this Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any information obtained pursuant to section 11,
    15, 16 or 114;

  - [(b.1)]{.lawlabel} any information obtained under any of sections
    53.71 to 53.81 of the [Canada Transportation Act](/eng/acts/C-10.4);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} whether notice has been given or information
    supplied in respect of a particular proposed transaction under
    section 114;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any information obtained from a person requesting a
    certificate under section 102; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any information provided voluntarily pursuant to
    this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} This section does not apply in respect of any
  information that has been made public or any information the
  communication of which was authorized by the person who provided the
  information.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-34, s. 29
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 26
- 2002, c. 16, s. 2.1
- [2018, c. 10, s. 83](#1037845-1122461){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1037845-1122461.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-29-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 29"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Communication to Minister of Transport

- **[[29.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection 29(1),
  the Commissioner may, if requested to do so by the Minister of
  Transport in accordance with subsection (3), communicate or allow to
  be communicated to that Minister any information referred to in
  subsection (2) that is specifically requested by that Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The information that may be communicated under this
  section is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the identity of any person from whom information
    was obtained under this Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any information obtained in the course of an
    inquiry under section 10 or 10.1;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any information obtained under section 11, 15, 16
    or 114;

  - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} any information obtained under any of sections
    53.71 to 53.81 of the [Canada Transportation Act](/eng/acts/C-10.4);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any information obtained from a person requesting a
    certificate under section 102;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} whether notice has been given or information
    supplied in respect of a particular proposed transaction under
    section 114; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} any information collected, received or generated by
    or on behalf of the Commissioner, including compilations and
    analyses.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of request

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Requests under this section must be in writing and
  must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} specify the information referred to in any of
    paragraphs (2)(a) to (f) that is required; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} state that the Minister of Transport requires the
    information for the purposes of section 53.1 or 53.2 or any of
    sections 53.71 to 53.81 of the [Canada Transportation
    Act](/eng/acts/C-10.4) and identify the transaction being considered
    under that section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The information communicated under subsection (1) may
  be used only for the purposes of section 53.1 or 53.2 or any of
  sections 53.71 to 53.81, as the case may be, of the [Canada
  Transportation Act](/eng/acts/C-10.4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Confidentiality

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} No person who performs or has performed duties or
  functions in the administration or enforcement of the [Canada
  Transportation Act](/eng/acts/C-10.4) shall communicate or allow to be
  communicated to any other person any information communicated under
  subsection (1), except to persons who perform duties or functions
  under section 53.1 or 53.2 or any of sections 53.71 to 53.81 of that
  Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2000, c. 15, s. 12
- 2007, c. 19, s. 61
- [2018, c. 10, s. 84](#1037858-1122475){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1037858-1122475.html"}
- [2023, c. 31, s. 6](#1431659-1432208){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1431659-1432208.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-29.1-20190403.html "Link to previous version of section 29.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Communication to Minister of Finance

- **[[29.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection 29(1),
  the Commissioner may, if requested to do so by the Minister of Finance
  in accordance with subsection (3), communicate or allow to be
  communicated to the Minister of Finance any information referred to in
  subsection (2) that is specifically requested by the Minister of
  Finance.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The information that may be communicated under this
  section is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the identity of any person from whom information
    was obtained under this Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any information obtained in the course of an
    inquiry under section 10 or 10.1;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any information obtained under section 11, 15, 16
    or 114;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any information obtained from a person requesting a
    certificate under section 102;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} whether notice has been given or information
    supplied in respect of a particular proposed transaction under
    section 114; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} any information collected, received or generated by
    or on behalf of the Commissioner, including compilations and
    analyses.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of request

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Requests under this section must be in writing and
  must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} specify the information referred to in any of
    paragraphs (2)(a) to (f) that is required;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} state that the Minister of Finance requires the
    information

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to consider a merger or proposed merger under the
      [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01), the [Cooperative Credit Associations
      Act](/eng/acts/C-41.01), the [Insurance Companies
      Act](/eng/acts/I-11.8) or the [Trust and Loan Companies
      Act](/eng/acts/T-19.8), or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to permit the Minister of Finance to determine
      whether he or she should provide the Commissioner with a
      certificate described in paragraph 94(b) in respect of such a
      merger or proposed merger;

    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} identify the merger or proposed merger.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The information communicated under subsection (1) may
  be used only for the purpose of making a decision in respect of the
  merger or proposed merger.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Confidentiality

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} No person who performs or has performed duties or
  functions, in the administration or enforcement of the [Bank
  Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01), the [Cooperative Credit Associations
  Act](/eng/acts/C-41.01), the [Insurance Companies
  Act](/eng/acts/I-11.8) or the [Trust and Loan Companies
  Act](/eng/acts/T-19.8) shall communicate or allow to be communicated
  to any other person any information communicated under subsection (1),
  except to other persons who perform those duties or functions.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 578
- [2023, c. 31, s. 7](#1431661-1432210){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1431661-1432210.html"}
:::
