[Previous
Version](section-113-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 113"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Anti-avoidance]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1366510 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of sections 114 to 123.1

**[[113.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-113.1 .sectionLabel}** If a transaction or
proposed transaction is designed to avoid the application of this Part,
sections 114 to 123.1 apply to the substance of the transaction or
proposed transaction.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 271](#1357379-1366514){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357379-1366514.html"}
:::

### [Notice and Information]{.HTitleText2} {#h-90168 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of proposed transaction

- **[[114]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-114
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, the parties to
  a proposed transaction shall, before the transaction is completed,
  notify the Commissioner that the transaction is proposed and supply
  the Commissioner with the prescribed information in accordance with
  this Part, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person, or two or more persons pursuant to an
    agreement or arrangement, propose to acquire assets in the
    circumstances set out in subsection 110(2), to acquire shares in the
    circumstances set out in subsection 110(3) or to acquire an interest
    in a combination in the circumstances set out in subsection 110(6);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} two or more entities propose to amalgamate in the
    circumstances set out in subsection 110(4); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} two or more persons propose to form a combination
    in the circumstances set out in subsection 110(5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner or a person authorized by the
  Commissioner may, within 30 days after receiving the prescribed
  information, send a notice to the person who supplied the information
  requiring them to supply additional information that is relevant to
  the Commissioner's assessment of the proposed transaction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of notice

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} The notice shall specify the particular additional
  information or classes of additional information that are to be
  supplied.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unsolicited bid

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a proposed transaction is an unsolicited or
  hostile take-over bid in respect of an entity and the Commissioner
  receives prescribed information supplied under subsection (1) by a
  person who has commenced or has announced an intention to commence a
  take-over bid, the Commissioner shall, if he or she has not already
  received the prescribed information from the entity, immediately
  notify the entity that the Commissioner has received the prescribed
  information from that person and the entity shall supply the
  Commissioner with the prescribed information within 10 days after
  being so notified.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice and information

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Any of the persons required to give notice and supply
  information under this section may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if duly authorized to do so, give notice or supply
    information on behalf of and in lieu of any of the others who are so
    required in respect of the same transaction; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} give notice or supply information jointly with any
    of those others.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 45
- 1999, c. 2, s. 31, c. 31, s. 53(F)
- 2009, c. 2, s. 437
- 2018, c. 8, s. 120
- [2022, c. 10, s. 272](#1357381-1366517){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357381-1366517.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-114-20180501.html "Link to previous version of section 114"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prior notice of acquisitions

- **[[115]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-115 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} It
  is not necessary to comply with section 114 in respect of a proposed
  acquisition of voting shares or of an interest in a combination where
  a limit set out in subsection 110(3) or (6) would be exceeded as a
  result of the proposed acquisition within three years immediately
  following a previous compliance with section 114 required in relation
  to the same limit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of future acquisition

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a person or persons who propose to acquire
  voting shares or an interest in a combination are required to comply
  with section 114 because the twenty or thirty-five per cent limit set
  out in subsection 110(3) or the thirty-five per cent limit set out in
  subsection 110(6) would be exceeded as a result of the acquisition,
  the person or persons may, at the time of the compliance, give notice
  to the Commissioner of a proposed further acquisition of voting shares
  or of an interest in a combination that would result in a fifty per
  cent limit set out in that subsection being exceeded, and supply the
  Commissioner with a detailed description in writing of the steps to be
  carried out in the further acquisition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption for further acquisitions of
  voting shares

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} It is not necessary to comply with section 114 in
  respect of a proposed further acquisition referred to in
  subsection (2) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} notice of the further acquisition is given to the
    Commissioner under subsection (2) and it is carried out in
    accordance with the description supplied under that subsection; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an additional notice of the further acquisition is
    given to the Commissioner in writing within twenty-one, and at least
    seven, days before the further acquisition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (3) does not apply in respect of a further
  acquisition unless the further acquisition is completed within one
  year after notice of it is given under subsection (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 45
- 1999, c. 2, ss. 32, 37
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If information cannot be supplied

- **[[116]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-116 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  any of the information required under section 114 is not known or
  reasonably obtainable, or cannot be supplied because of the privilege
  that exists in respect of lawyers and notaries and their clients or
  because of a confidentiality requirement established by law, the
  entity or individual who is supplying the information may, instead of
  supplying the information, inform the Commissioner under oath or
  solemn affirmation of the matters in respect of which information has
  not been supplied and the reason why it has not been supplied.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If information not relevant

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If any of the information required under section 114
  could not, on any reasonable basis, be considered to be relevant to an
  assessment by the Commissioner as to whether the proposed transaction
  would or would be likely to prevent or lessen competition
  substantially, the entity or individual who is supplying the
  information may, instead of supplying the information, inform the
  Commissioner under oath or solemn affirmation of the matters in
  respect of which information has not been supplied and why the
  information was not considered relevant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If information previously supplied

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} If any of the information required under section
  114 has previously been supplied to the Commissioner, the entity or
  individual who is supplying the information may, instead of supplying
  it, inform the Commissioner under oath or solemn affirmation of the
  matters in respect of which information has previously been supplied
  and when it was supplied.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Commissioner may require information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an entity or individual chooses not to supply the
  Commissioner with information required under section 114 and so
  informs the Commissioner in accordance with subsection (2) or (2.1)
  and the Commissioner or a person authorized by the Commissioner
  notifies that entity or individual, within seven days after the
  Commissioner is so informed, that the information is required, the
  entity or individual shall supply the Commissioner with the
  information.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 45
- 1999, c. 2, ss. 33, 37
- 2009, c. 2, s. 438
- 2018, c. 8, s. 121
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-116-20090312.html "Link to previous version of section 116"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Saving

- **[[117]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-117
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in section 114 requires

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any individual who is a director of a corporation
    to supply information that is known to that individual by virtue
    only of their position as a director of an affiliate of the
    corporation that is neither a wholly-owned affiliate nor a
    wholly-owning affiliate of the corporation; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any individual who, in respect of an entity other
    than a corporation, serves in a capacity similar to that of a
    director to supply information that is known to that individual by
    virtue only of their serving in that capacity with respect to an
    affiliate of the entity that is neither a wholly-owned affiliate nor
    a wholly-owning affiliate of the entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Wholly-owned affiliate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), one corporation
  is the wholly-owned affiliate of another corporation if all its
  outstanding voting shares, other than shares necessary to qualify
  persons as directors, are beneficially owned by that other corporation
  directly, or indirectly through one or more affiliates where all the
  outstanding voting shares of the affiliates, other than shares
  necessary to qualify persons as directors, are beneficially owned by
  that other corporation or each other.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Wholly-owning affiliate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), one corporation
  is the wholly-owning affiliate of another corporation if it
  beneficially owns all the outstanding voting shares of that other
  corporation, other than shares necessary to qualify persons as
  directors, directly, or indirectly through one or more affiliates
  where all the outstanding voting shares of the affiliates, other than
  shares necessary to qualify persons as directors, are beneficially
  owned by the corporation or each other.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 45
- 2018, c. 8, s. 122
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-117-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 117"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information to be certified

**[[118]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-118 .sectionLabel}** The information
supplied to the Commissioner under section 114 shall be certified on
oath or solemn affirmation as having been examined by one of the
following individuals and as being, to the best of that individual's
knowledge and belief, correct and complete in all material respects:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a corporation supplying the
  information, by an officer of the corporation or other person duly
  authorized by the board of directors or other governing body of the
  corporation;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an entity other than a corporation
  supplying the information, by an individual who serves in a capacity
  similar to that of an officer of a corporation or other individual
  duly authorized by the governing body of that entity;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an individual supplying the
  information, by that individual.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 45
- 1999, c. 2, s. 37
- 2018, c. 8, s. 123
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-118-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 118"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where transaction not completed

**[[119]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-119 .sectionLabel}** Where notice is given
and information supplied in respect of a proposed transaction under
section 114 but the transaction is not completed within one year
thereafter or such longer period as the Commissioner may specify in any
particular case, section 114 applies as if no notice were given or
information supplied.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 45
- 1999, c. 2, s. 37
:::

**[[120 to 122]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-120_to_122
.sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 1999, c. 2, s. 34\]]{.Repealed}

### [Completion of Proposed Transactions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-90234 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time when transaction may not proceed

- **[[123]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-123 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  proposed transaction referred to in section 114 shall not be completed
  before the end of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 30 days after the day on which the information
    required under subsection 114(1) has been received by the
    Commissioner, if the Commissioner has not, within that time,
    required additional information to be supplied under subsection
    114(2); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 30 days after the day on which the information
    required under subsection 114(2) has been received by the
    Commissioner, if the Commissioner has within the 30-day period
    referred to in paragraph (a) required additional information to be
    supplied under subsection 114(2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Waiving of waiting period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A proposed transaction referred to in section 114 may
  be completed before the end of a period referred to in subsection (1)
  if, before the end of that period, the Commissioner or a person
  authorized by the Commissioner notifies the parties to the transaction
  that the Commissioner does not, at that time, intend to make an
  application under section 92 in respect of that proposed transaction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition of equity interests

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of an acquisition of equity interests to
  which subsection 114(3) applies, the periods referred to in
  subsection (1) shall be determined without reference to the day on
  which the information required under section 114 is received by the
  Commissioner from the entity whose equity interests are being
  acquired.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 45
- 1999, c. 2, s. 35
- 2009, c. 2, s. 439
- 2018, c. 8, s. 124
- [2022, c. 10, s. 273(E)](#1357383-1366519){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357383-1366519.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-123-20180501.html "Link to previous version of section 123"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Failure to comply

- **[[123.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-123.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If, on application by the
  Commissioner, the court determines that a person, without good and
  sufficient cause, the proof of which lies on the person, has completed
  or is likely to complete a proposed transaction before the end of the
  applicable period referred to in section 123 or without having given
  the notice or information required under subsection 114(1), the court
  may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} order the person to submit information required
    under subsection 114(2);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} issue an interim order prohibiting any person from
    doing anything that it appears to the court may constitute or be
    directed toward the completion or implementation of the proposed
    transaction;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a completed transaction, order any
    party to the transaction or any other person, in any manner that the
    court directs, to dissolve the merger or to dispose of assets or
    shares designated by the court;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a completed transaction, order the
    person to pay, in any manner that the court specifies, an
    administrative monetary penalty in an amount not exceeding \$10,000
    for each day on which they have failed to comply with section 123,
    determined by the court after taking into account any evidence of
    the following:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person's financial position,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person's history of compliance with this
      Act,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the duration of the period of non-compliance,
      and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} any other relevant factor; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} grant any other relief that the court considers
    appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purpose of order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The terms of an order under paragraph (1)(d) shall be
  determined with a view to promoting conduct by the person that is in
  conformity with the purposes of this Part and not with a view to
  punishment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unpaid monetary penalty

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The amount of an administrative monetary penalty
  imposed under paragraph (1)(d) is a debt due to Her Majesty in right
  of Canada and may be recovered as such from the person in a court of
  competent jurisdiction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[court]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[court]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
  the Tribunal, the Federal Court or the superior court of a province.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 2, s. 439
- [2024, c. 15, s. 263](#1462940-1483974){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1462940-1483974.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-123.1-20090312.html "Link to previous version of section 123.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Regulations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-90266 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[124]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-124 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may make regulations prescribing anything that is
  by this Part to be prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication of proposed regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), a copy of each regulation
  that the Governor in Council proposes to make under subsection (1)
  shall be published in the [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/)
  at least sixty days before the proposed effective date thereof and a
  reasonable opportunity shall be afforded to interested persons to make
  representations with respect thereto.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No proposed regulation need be published under
  subsection (2) if it has previously been published pursuant to that
  subsection, whether or not it has been amended as a result of
  representations made pursuant to that subsection.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 45
:::

## [PART X]{.HLabel1}[General]{.HTitleText1} {#h-90276 .Part}

### [Commissioner's Opinions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-90277 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for written opinion

- **[[124.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-124.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any person may apply to the
  Commissioner, with supporting information, for an opinion on the
  applicability of any provision of this Act or the regulations to
  conduct or a practice that the applicant proposes to engage in, and
  the Commissioner may provide a written opinion for the applicant's
  guidance.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Opinion binding

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If all the material facts have been submitted by or
  on behalf of an applicant for an opinion and they are accurate, a
  written opinion provided under this section is binding on the
  Commissioner. It remains binding for so long as the material facts on
  which the opinion was based remain substantially unchanged and the
  conduct or practice is carried out substantially as proposed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 16, s. 15
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-124.1-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 124.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [References to Tribunal]{.HTitleText2} {#h-90285 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reference if parties agree

- **[[124.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-124.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner and a person who is
  the subject of an inquiry under section 10 or 10.1 may by agreement
  refer to the Tribunal for determination any question of law, mixed law
  and fact, jurisdiction, practice or procedure, in relation to the
  application or interpretation of Part VII.1 or VIII, whether or not an
  application has been made under Part VII.1 or VIII.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reference by Commissioner

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner may, at any time, refer to the
  Tribunal for determination a question of law, jurisdiction, practice
  or procedure, in relation to the application or interpretation of
  Parts VII.1 to IX.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reference by agreement of parties to a
  private action

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person granted leave under section 103.1 and the
  person against whom an order is sought under section 74.1, 75, 76, 77,
  79 or 90.1 may by agreement refer to the Tribunal for determination
  any question of law, or mixed law and fact, in relation to the
  application or interpretation of Part VII.1 or VIII, if the Tribunal
  grants them leave. They must send a notice of their application for
  leave to the Commissioner, who may intervene in the proceedings.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reference procedure

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Tribunal shall decide the questions referred to
  it informally and expeditiously, in accordance with any rules on
  references made under section 16 of the [Competition Tribunal
  Act](/eng/acts/C-36.4).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 16, s. 15
- 2015, c. 3, s. 41
- [2022, c. 10, s. 274](#1357385-1366522){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357385-1366522.html"}
- [2023, c. 31, s. 11](#1431683-1432237){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1431683-1432237.html"}
- [2024, c. 15, s. 264](#1462942-1532087){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1462942-1532087.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-124.2-20231215.html "Link to previous version of section 124.2"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Agreements and Arrangements Related to Protecting the Environment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1483975 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate

- **[[124.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-124.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the Commissioner is satisfied by
  a party or parties that propose to enter into an agreement or
  arrangement that it is for the purpose of protecting the environment
  and that it is not likely to prevent or lessen competition
  substantially in a market, they may issue a certificate that they are
  so satisfied.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty of Commissioner

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner must consider any request for a
  certificate under this section as soon as practicable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty of party or parties

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The party or parties seeking a certificate must, on
  request, provide to the Commissioner any information related to the
  agreement or arrangement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of certificate

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner must specify in the certificate the
  names of the parties to the agreement or arrangement as well as a
  description of the agreement or arrangement's content.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner may specify in the certificate any
  terms that the Commissioner considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of validity

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner must specify in the certificate its
  period of validity, which is not to exceed 10 years, and on request of
  the parties may extend that period for one or more additional periods
  not exceeding 10 years.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2024, c. 15, s. 265](#1462944-1483990){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1462944-1483990.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration

**[[124.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-124.4 .sectionLabel}** The Commissioner
must file a certificate issued under subsection 124.3(1) with the
Tribunal for immediate registration.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2024, c. 15, s. 265](#1462944-1483994){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1462944-1483994.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of sections 45, 46, 47,
49 and 90.1

**[[124.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-124.5 .sectionLabel}** Sections 45, 46,
47, 49 and 90.1 do not apply in respect of an agreement or arrangement
that is the subject of a certificate issued under subsection 124.3(1)
that is valid and registered.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2024, c. 15, s. 265](#1462944-1483998){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1462944-1483998.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of termination

- **[[124.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-124.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The parties to an agreement or
  arrangement that is the subject of a valid certificate issued under
  subsection 124.3(1) must, within 15 days of the day on which they
  terminate the agreement or arrangement, give notice of the termination
  to the Commissioner and the Tribunal.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rescission of certificate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Tribunal must, without delay after receiving the
  notice, rescind the certificate.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2024, c. 15, s. 265](#1462944-1484005){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1462944-1484005.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rescission or variation of certificate

**[[124.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-124.7 .sectionLabel}** The Tribunal may
rescind or vary a certificate issued under subsection 124.3(1) if, on
application by the Commissioner, the parties to the agreement or
arrangement that is the subject of the certificate or a person directly
and substantially affected in the whole or part of their business by the
agreement or arrangement, the Tribunal finds that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the parties have terminated the agreement or
  arrangement without giving notice of the termination in accordance
  with subsection 124.6(1);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the parties have agreed, with the Commissioner's
  consent, to vary the agreement or arrangement;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the agreement or arrangement is not being implemented
  in accordance with the description of it in the certificate;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the parties have failed to comply with the terms
  specified in the certificate; or

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the agreement or arrangement prevents or lessens, or
  is likely to prevent or lessen, competition substantially in a market.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2024, c. 15, s. 265](#1462944-1484014){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1462944-1484014.html"}
:::

### [Representations to Boards, Commissions or Other Tribunals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-90297 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations to federal boards, etc.

- **[[125]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-125 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commissioner, at the request of any federal board, commission or other
  tribunal or on his own initiative, may, and on direction from the
  Minister shall, make representations to and call evidence before the
  board, commission or other tribunal in respect of competition,
  whenever such representations are, or evidence is, relevant to a
  matter before the board, commission or other tribunal, and to the
  factors that the board, commission or other tribunal is entitled to
  take into consideration in determining the matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[federal board,
  commission or other tribunal]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, [[federal board,
  commission or other tribunal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any board,
  commission, tribunal or person that carries on regulatory activities
  and is expressly charged by or pursuant to an enactment of Parliament
  with the responsibility of making decisions or recommendations related
  directly or indirectly to the production, supply, acquisition or
  distribution of a product.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 45
- 1999, c. 2, s. 37
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations to provincial boards,
etc.

- **[[126]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-126 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commissioner, at the request of any provincial board, commission or
  other tribunal, or on his own initiative with the consent of the
  board, commission or other tribunal, may make representations to and
  call evidence before the board, commission or other tribunal in
  respect of competition, whenever such representations are, or evidence
  is, relevant to a matter before the board, commission or other
  tribunal, and to the factors that the board, commission or other
  tribunal is entitled to take into consideration in determining the
  matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[provincial board,
  commission or other tribunal]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, [[provincial board,
  commission or other tribunal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any board,
  commission, tribunal or person that carries on regulatory activities
  and is expressly charged by or pursuant to an enactment of the
  legislature of a province with the responsibility of making decisions
  or recommendations related directly or indirectly to the production,
  supply, acquisition or distribution of a product.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 45
- 1999, c. 2, s. 37
:::

### [Report to Parliament]{.HTitleText2} {#h-90312 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Annual report

**[[127]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-127 .sectionLabel}** The Commissioner shall
report annually to the Minister on the operation of the Acts referred to
in subsection 7(1), and the Minister shall cause the report to be laid
before each House of Parliament on any of the first fifteen days after
the Minister receives the report on which that House is sitting.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 45
- 1999, c. 2, s. 36
:::

### [Regulations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-90317 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[128]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-128 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may make such regulations as are necessary for
  carrying out this Act and for the efficient administration thereof.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication of proposed regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), a copy of each regulation
  that the Governor in Council proposes to make under subsection (1)
  shall be published in the [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/)
  at least sixty days before the proposed effective date thereof and a
  reasonable opportunity shall be afforded to interested persons to make
  representations with respect thereto.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No proposed regulation need be published under
  subsection (2) if it has previously been published pursuant to that
  subsection, whether or not it has been amended as a result of
  representations made pursuant to that subsection.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 45
:::

## RELATED PROVISIONS {#related-provisions .scheduleTitleText}

- --- R.S., 1985, c. 19 (2nd Supp.), s. 61

  - ###### Orders of the Commission {#orders-of-the-commission .MarginalNote}

    [61]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of the [Competition
    Act](/eng/acts/C-34), as amended by this Act, an order of the
    Restrictive Trade Practices Commission under Part V, as it read
    immediately prior to the coming into force of section 29 of this
    Act, or pursuant to subsection 60(1) shall be deemed to be an order
    of the Competition Tribunal under the [Competition
    Act](/eng/acts/C-34).

- --- 1999, c. 2, ss. 38 to 40

  - ###### Person holding office of Director {#person-holding-office-of-director .MarginalNote}

    - [38]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The person holding the office of
      Director of Investigation and Research immediately before the
      coming into force of section 4 shall continue in office as the
      Commissioner of Competition referred to in section 7 of the
      [Competition Act](/eng/acts/C-34), as amended by this Act.

    - ###### Persons holding office as Deputy Director {#persons-holding-office-as-deputy-director .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person holding the office of Deputy
      Director of Investigation and Research immediately before the
      coming into force of section 5 shall continue in office as a
      Deputy Commissioner of Competition referred to in section 8 of the
      [Competition Act](/eng/acts/C-34), as enacted by this Act.

- --- 1999, c. 2, ss. 38 to 40

  - ###### Outstanding prohibition orders {#outstanding-prohibition-orders .MarginalNote}

    [39]{.lawlabel} An order made under section 34 of the [Competition
    Act](/eng/acts/C-34) in respect of an offence under any of sections
    52, 53 or 57 to 59 of that Act, as those sections read immediately
    before the coming into force of sections 12, 14 and 17 of this Act,
    is deemed to have been made under paragraph 74.1(1)(a) of the
    [Competition Act](/eng/acts/C-34), as enacted by section 22 of this
    Act.

- --- 1999, c. 2, ss. 38 to 40

  - ###### Variation or rescission of orders {#variation-or-rescission-of-orders .MarginalNote}

    [40]{.lawlabel} Subsection 34(2.3) of the [Competition
    Act](/eng/acts/C-34), as enacted by subsection 11(2) of this Act,
    applies in respect of orders made under section 34 of the
    [Competition Act](/eng/acts/C-34) whether before or after the coming
    into force of section 11 of this Act.

- --- 1999, c. 2, s. 54

  - ###### References to "Director" {#references-to-director .MarginalNote}

    [54]{.lawlabel} Every reference to the Director of Investigation and
    Research or a Deputy Director of Investigation and Research in any
    other Act of Parliament or in a regulation, order or other
    instrument made under any Act of Parliament is deemed to be a
    reference to the Commissioner of Competition or a Deputy
    Commissioner of Competition, as the case may be.

- --- 2009, c. 2, s. 440

  - ###### Agreements or arrangements entered into before royal assent {#agreements-or-arrangements-entered-into-before-royal-assent .MarginalNote}

    [440]{.lawlabel} Any party to an agreement or arrangement entered
    into before the day on which this Act receives royal assent may,
    within one year after that day, apply under section 124.1 of the
    [Competition Act](/eng/acts/C-34) without payment of any fee for an
    opinion on the applicability to the agreement or arrangement of
    section 45 or 90.1 of the [Competition Act](/eng/acts/C-34), as
    enacted by sections 410 and 429, respectively, as if the agreement
    or arrangement had not yet been entered into and as if that section
    45 or 90.1 were in force.

- --- 2023, c. 31, s. 12

  - ###### Sections 92 and 96 of the [Competition Act](/eng/acts/C-34) {#sections-92-and-96-of-the-competition-act .MarginalNote}

    [12]{.lawlabel} Sections 92 and 96 of the [Competition
    Act](/eng/acts/C-34), as they read before the day on which sections
    9 and 10 come into force, continue to apply after that day to a
    proposed transaction notified under section 114 of that Act before
    that day or to a merger that has been substantially completed before
    that day.

- --- 2024, c. 15, s. 267

  - ###### Subsection 67(4) of the [Competition Act](/eng/acts/C-34) {#subsection-674-of-the-competition-act .MarginalNote}

    [267]{.lawlabel} Subsection 67(4) of the [Competition
    Act](/eng/acts/C-34), as enacted by section 235, applies only in
    respect of corporations that are charged with an offence under that
    Act on or after the day on which this Act receives royal assent.

- --- 2024, c. 15, s. 268

  - ###### Subsection 92(2) of the [Competition Act](/eng/acts/C-34) {#subsection-922-of-the-competition-act .MarginalNote}

    [268]{.lawlabel} Subsection 92(2) of the [Competition
    Act](/eng/acts/C-34), as that subsection read before the day on
    which subsection 249(2) comes into force, continues to apply after
    that day in respect of a proposed transaction that was the subject
    of a notification provided under section 114 of that Act before that
    day or to a merger that is substantially completed before that day.
